61	function collideGround(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks): void {
62	  const st = game.world.store;
63	  const tx = Math.floor(e.cx / TILE);
64	  const ty = Math.floor((e.y + e.h) / TILE);
65	  if (st.inBounds(tx, ty) && st.isSolid(tx, ty) && e.vy >= 0) {
66	    e.y = ty * TILE - e.h;
67	    e.vy = 0;
68	    e.onGround = true;
69	  } else {
70	    e.onGround = false;
71	  }
72	  const ty2 = Math.floor(e.y / TILE);
73	  if (st.inBounds(tx, ty2) && st.isSolid(tx, ty2) && e.vy < 0) {
74	    e.y = (ty2 + 1) * TILE;
75	    e.vy = 0;
76	  }
77	  const tx2 = Math.floor((e.x + e.w) / TILE);
78	  if (st.inBounds(tx2, Math.floor(e.cy / TILE)) && st.isSolid(tx2, Math.floor(e.cy / TILE)) && e.vx > 0) {
79	    e.x = tx2 * TILE - e.w; e.vx = 0;
80	  }
81	  const tx3 = Math.floor(e.x / TILE);
82	  if (st.inBounds(tx3, Math.floor(e.cy / TILE)) && st.isSolid(tx3, Math.floor(e.cy / TILE)) && e.vx < 0) {
83	    e.x = (tx3 + 1) * TILE; e.vx = 0;
84	  }
85	}

88	function aimLead(e: Enemy, player: Player, fallbackX: number): { x: number; y: number } {
89	  const dx = player.cx + player.vx * 20 - e.cx;
90	  const dy = player.cy + player.vy * 20 - e.cy;
91	  const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
92	  if (!Number.isFinite(d) || d < 0.0001) return { x: fallbackX, y: 0 };
93	  return { x: dx / d, y: dy / d };
94	}

112	function cultistPick(e: Enemy, phase2: boolean): number {
113	  const table = phase2 ? CULTIST_CYCLE_P2 : CULTIST_CYCLE_P1;
114	  const i = Math.floor(e.ai3);
115	  if (i < 0 || i >= table.length) { e.ai3 = -1; return 0; }

117	  return table[i];

120	export function lunaticCultistAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null): void {
121	  const la = laOf(e);
122	  if (!e.bInit) {
123	    // :65047-65057 localAI[0]==0:音效 + alpha=255 + ai[0]=-1(出生显形)

126	    la.la0 = 1;

129	  }
130	  const b = baseOf(e);

132	  e.def.defense = Math.floor(phase2 ? b.def * 0.65 : b.def); // :64982-64985 半血防御 ×0.65
133	
134	  // :65060-65088 玩家死/超 5600px → 消散退场
135	  if (!player || player.dead || Math.hypot(player.cx - e.cx, player.cy - e.cy) > 5600) {
136	    game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy, '#6040A0', 14, 2.4);
137	    e.dead = true;
138	    return;
139	  }
140	  // 朝向(:65117-65120 sign(player.X - center.X))
141	  const face = Math.sign(player.cx - e.cx);
142	  if (face !== 0) e.facing = face;
143	
144	  // ---- ai0 == -1 出生显形(:65059-65109):420t,前段不可选中;300t 起上浮 ----
145	  if (e.ai0 === -1) {
146	    e.spawnAlpha = Math.max(0, 254 - e.ai1 * 5); // :65066 alpha -= 5/tick
147	    e.iframes = 2; // flag3/flag4 → dontTakeDamage
148	    e.ai1 += 1;
149	    if (e.ai1 > 360) { e.vx *= 0.95; e.vy *= 0.95; }      // :65117-65123 收势阻尼
150	    else if (e.ai1 > 300) { e.vx = 0; e.vy = -1; }        // :65126-65130 velocity=-UnitY
151	    flyIntegrate(e);
152	    if (e.ai1 >= 420) { e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; e.spawnAlpha = 0; }
153	    return;
154	  }
155	
156	  // ---- ai0 == 0 选招(:65110-65336):40t 后按 ai3 拍号取招 ----
157	  if (e.ai0 === 0) {
158	    e.ai1 += 1;
159	    if (e.ai1 >= 40) {
160	      e.ai1 = 0;
161	      const pick = cultistPick(e, phase2);
162	      if (pick === 0) {
163	        // 飞掠占位(:65277-65336):无克隆体时环位恒为玩家正上 (0,-200)(num16=0),
164	        // num14 = ceil(距离/50) 至少 1;velocity = 位移/num14,ai1 = num14*2(隔拍移动)
165	        const destX = player.cx;
166	        const destY = player.cy - 200;
167	        const dist = Math.hypot(destX - e.cx, destY - e.cy);
168	        const num14 = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(dist / 50));
169	        e.vx = (destX - e.cx) / num14;
170	        e.vy = (destY - e.cy) / num14;
171	        e.x -= e.vx; e.y -= e.vy; // :65318-65321 whoAmI>=自身 → 选拍当帧回退(净位移 0)
172	        e.ai0 = 1;
173	        e.ai1 = num14 * 2;
174	      } else if (pick === 1) { e.ai0 = 3; } // 火球
175	      else if (pick === 2) { e.ai0 = 2; }   // 冰雾
176	      else if (pick === 3) { e.ai0 = 4; }   // 闪电
177	      else if (pick === 4) { e.ai0 = 5; }   // 仪式瞬移
178	      else if (pick === 5) { e.ai0 = 7; }   // 远古之光
179	      // pick === 6(远古末日)仅 expert 随机替换会命中(:65270-65275),经典不触发
180	    }
181	    flyIntegrate(e);
182	    return;
183	  }
184	
185	  // ---- ai0 == 1 飞掠(:65338-65361):奇数拍回退位移 → 实际隔拍移动;期间无敌(flag3) ----
186	  if (e.ai0 === 1) {
187	    e.iframes = 2;
188	    const undo = Math.floor(e.ai1) % 2 !== 0 && e.ai1 !== 1; // :65341-65344
189	    if (!undo) flyIntegrate(e);
190	    e.ai1 -= 1;
191	    if (e.ai1 <= 0) { e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai3 += 1; e.vx = 0; e.vy = 0; }
192	    return;
193	  }
194	
195	  // ---- ai0 == 2 冰雾 464(:65362-65429):4t 时一发,35 伤,4 速直飞;持续 4+120 ----
196	  if (e.ai0 === 2) {
197	    if (e.ai1 >= 4 && (e.ai1 - 4) % 120 === 0) {
198	      const aim = aimLead(e, player, e.facing);
199	      const [ox, oy] = [e.cx + e.facing * 30, e.cy + 12];
200	      shoot(game, ox, oy, aim.x * 4, aim.y * 4, 35, 464);
201	      game.playSfx('roar', 0.35);
202	    }
203	    e.ai1 += 1;
204	    if (e.ai1 >= 4 + 120) { e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai3 += 1; e.vx = 0; e.vy = 0; }
205	    return;
206	  }
207	
208	  // ---- ai0 == 3 火球 467(:65430-65530):每 18t 一发×3,30 伤,(6+rand4) 速 ±0.52rad 散布;持续 4+18*3 ----
209	  if (e.ai0 === 3) {
210	    if (e.ai1 >= 4 && (e.ai1 - 4) % 18 === 0) {
211	      const aim = aimLead(e, player, e.facing);
212	      const spd = 6 + Math.random() * 4;
213	      const [dvx, dvy] = rot(aim.x * spd, aim.y * spd, (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2 * 0.5235987901687622);
214	      shoot(game, e.cx + e.facing * 30, e.cy + 12, dvx, dvy, 30, 467);
215	    }
216	    // 第三拍(num13==2)克隆体补射 468(:65462-65499)——无克隆体,跳过
217	    e.ai1 += 1;
218	    if (e.ai1 >= 4 + 18 * 3) { e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai3 += 1; e.vx = 0; e.vy = 0; }
219	    return;
220	  }
221	
222	  // ---- ai0 == 4 闪电 465(:65531-65622):20t 时在头顶 100px 撒球,45 伤;持续 20+80 ----
223	  if (e.ai0 === 4) {
224	    if (e.ai1 === 20) {
225	      // 原版 465 零速悬停后从天引雷(Projectile.AI 收敛:直飞 6 速朝玩家)
226	      const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - (e.cy - 100);
227	      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
228	      shoot(game, e.cx, e.cy - 100, dx / d * 6, dy / d * 6, 45, 465);
229	      game.playSfx('roar', 0.5);
230	    }
231	    e.ai1 += 1;
232	    if (e.ai1 >= 20 + 80) { e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai3 += 1; e.vx = 0; e.vy = 0; }
233	    return;
234	  }
235	
236	  // ---- ai0 == 5 仪式瞬移(:65623-65756):0-30 淡出,30 瞬移,30-90 隐形,90-120 淡入,
237	  //      120-420 可被选中(打真身窗口);持续 420 ----
238	  if (e.ai0 === 5) {
239	    const t = e.ai1;
240	    if (t >= 0 && t < 30) {
241	      e.spawnAlpha = Math.floor((t / 30) * 254);
242	      e.iframes = 2; // flag3/flag4
243	    } else if (t >= 30 && t < 90) {
244	      e.spawnAlpha = 254;
245	      e.iframes = 2;
246	      if (t === 30) {
247	        // :65636-65648 原版为绕自身半径 180 的环位重定位(≤180px);
248	        // 收敛:直接瞬移到绕玩家椭圆 (300,200) 8 环位中最远一格——即下一拍飞掠的目的地,
249	        // 保留「消失→别处再现」语义且产生 >300px 位置跳变。
250	        let far = 0, farD = -1;
251	        for (let k = 0; k < 8; k++) {
252	          const a = (k / 8) * Math.PI * 2;
253	          const px = player.cx + Math.cos(a) * 300;
254	          const py = player.cy + Math.sin(a) * 200;
255	          const d = Math.hypot(px - e.cx, py - e.cy);
256	          if (d > farD) { farD = d; far = k; }
257	        }
258	        const a = (far / 8) * Math.PI * 2;
259	        e.x = player.cx + Math.cos(a) * 300 - e.w / 2;
260	        e.y = player.cy + Math.sin(a) * 200 - e.h / 2;
261	        e.vx = 0; e.vy = 0;
262	        game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy, '#8060D0', 18, 2.6);
263	        game.playSfx('roar', 0.7);
264	        // 克隆体(NPC 440,环位补位 + 490 仪式弹幕,:65636-65675)按任务要求跳过
265	      }
266	    } else if (t >= 90 && t < 120) {
267	      e.spawnAlpha = Math.floor(254 * (1 - (t - 90) / 30));
268	      e.iframes = 2;
269	    } else {
270	      e.spawnAlpha = 0; // 120-420 可选中(chaseable)
271	    }
272	    e.ai1 += 1;
273	    if (e.ai1 >= 420) { e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai3 += 1; e.vx = 0; e.vy = 0; }
274	    return;
275	  }
276	
277	  // ---- ai0 == 6 打中真身硬直(:65757-65770,克隆分支专用)——跳过 ----
278	
279	  // ---- ai0 == 7 远古之光(:65796-65877):每 20t 一轮×2,每轮 5 发呈扇面(步进 2π/25);持续 4+20*2 ----
280	  if (e.ai0 === 7) {
281	    if (e.ai1 >= 4 && (e.ai1 - 4) % 20 === 0) {
282	      e.facing = Math.sign(player.cx - e.cx) || e.facing; // :65842-65846
283	      const aim = aimLead(e, player, e.facing);
284	      const num47 = (Math.PI * 2) / 25; // :65856
285	      for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
286	        const [dvx, dvy] = rot(aim.x * 8, aim.y * 8, num47 * i - (Math.PI * 2 / 5 - num47) / 2); // :65860
287	        // 简化项:原版生成 5 只 NPC522 远古之光(:65862-65865,aiStyle 100 直飞核未移植,
288	        // 现分发会落 zombieAI),按同弹道收敛为 468 直飞弹,伤取同段克隆火球 18(:65837)
289	        shoot(game, e.cx + e.facing * 30, e.cy + 12 + 7, dvx, dvy, 18, 468);
290	      }
291	      game.playSfx('roar', 0.5);
292	    }
293	    e.ai1 += 1;
294	    if (e.ai1 >= 4 + 20 * 2) { e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai3 += 1; e.vx = 0; e.vy = 0; }
295	    return;
296	  }
297	
298	  // ---- ai0 == 8 远古末日(:65873-65932):玩家附近随机空位撒 NPC523(最多 1 只);持续 4+20*3 ----
299	  // 经典选招表不含 6(expert 随机替换专属,:65270-65275),此处仅保留状态体防外部置入。
300	  if (e.ai0 === 8) {
301	    if (e.ai1 >= 4 && (e.ai1 - 4) % 20 === 0) {
302	      const st = game.world.store;
303	      const ptx = Math.floor(player.cx / TILE), pty = Math.floor(player.cy / TILE);
304	      const etx = Math.floor(e.cx / TILE), ety = Math.floor(e.cy / TILE);
305	      for (let tries = 0; tries < 100; tries++) {
306	        const tx = ptx + Math.floor(Math.random() * 41) - 20;
307	        const ty = pty + Math.floor(Math.random() * 41) - 20;
308	        const nearPlayer = Math.abs(ty - pty) <= 7 && Math.abs(tx - ptx) <= 7;
309	        const nearSelf = Math.abs(ty - ety) <= 3 && Math.abs(tx - etx) <= 3;
310	        if (nearPlayer || nearSelf || !st.inBounds(tx, ty) || st.isSolid(tx, ty)) continue;
311	        const n = spawnPart(game, e, 523);
312	        if (n) { n.x = tx * TILE + 8 - n.w / 2; n.y = ty * TILE + 8 - n.h / 2; n.vx = 0; n.vy = 0; n.bInit = true; }
313	        break;
314	      }
315	    }
316	    e.ai1 += 1;
317	    if (e.ai1 >= 4 + 20 * 3) { e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai3 += 1; e.vx = 0; e.vy = 0; }
318	    return;
319	  }
320	
321	  // 未知状态:回选招
322	  e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0;
323	}
324	
325	// ===================== AI_120 光之女皇 636(NPC.cs:46299-47302) =====================
326	// 状态机(ai0): 0 出生(180t) / 1 悬浮蓄力枢纽(DashTo 靠近 10t + 减速,45t(二阶段 20t)选招)/
327	//   2 彩虹射线 873 / 4 光枪墙 919 / 5 永恒彩虹 872 / 6 太阳舞 923 / 7 光枪回环 919(二阶段)/
328	//   8,9 刀阵冲刺(±50 速,6-40t 无敌蓄力,40-90t 伤害 ×1.5)/ 10 半血变身传送 /
329	//   13 淡出退场(目标失效/超 6400px)。3(874 天降极光)与 11/12 为 expert 专属拍,跳过。
330	// 经典弹幕伤害(:46326-46345): 919 墙 50 / 919 回环 70 / 873 45 / 872 45 / 923 50 / 874 40。
331	// 选招表(:46400-46490): 一阶段 10 拍 [2,8,6,8,5,2,8,4,8,5] 循环,血 ≤50% 强制 10(变身);
332	//   二阶段 9 拍 [7,2,8,5,2,6,6,4,8] 循环(expert 追加 11/12 拍已剔除)。
333	const EMPRESS_CYCLE_P1 = [2, 8, 6, 8, 5, 2, 8, 4, 8, 5];
334	const EMPRESS_CYCLE_P2 = [7, 2, 8, 5, 2, 6, 6, 4, 8];
335	
336	/** 原版 AI_120_HallowBoss_DashTo(:47304-47317):目标上移 300,>200 再回退 100,
337	 *  速度 = lerp(单位向量*min(len,18), vector/6, lerpValue(100,600,len)) */
338	function empressDashTo(e: Enemy, tx0: number, ty0: number): void {
339	  let tx = tx0, ty = ty0 - 300;
340	  const d0 = Math.hypot(tx - e.cx, ty - e.cy);
341	  if (d0 > 200) {
342	    const ux = (tx - e.cx) / (d0 || 1), uy = (ty - e.cy) / (d0 || 1);
343	    tx -= ux * 100; ty -= uy * 100;
344	  }
345	  const vx = tx - e.cx, vy = ty - e.cy;
346	  const len = Math.hypot(vx, vy) || 1;
347	  const lv = lerpValue(100, 600, len);
348	  const cap = Math.min(len, 18);
349	  e.vx = vLerp((vx / len) * cap, vx / 6, lv);
350	  e.vy = vLerp((vy / len) * cap, vy / 6, lv);
351	}
352	
353	/** 悬浮趋近(:46502 系):距 (玩家+偏移) > 40 才以 SimpleFlyMovement(dir*12, 0.5) 靠近 */
354	function empressHover(e: Enemy, player: Player, ox: number, oy: number, spdMul = 1, accMul = 1): void {
355	  const tx = player.cx + ox, ty = player.cy + oy;
356	  if (Math.hypot(tx - e.cx, ty - e.cy) > 40) {
357	    const dx = tx - e.cx, dy = ty - e.cy;
358	    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
359	    simpleFly(e, (dx / d) * 12 * spdMul, (dy / d) * 12 * spdMul, 0.5 * accMul);
360	  }
361	}
362	
363	export function empressOfLightAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null): void {
364	  if (!e.bInit) {
365	    e.bInit = true;
366	    e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 = 0;
367	    e.spawnAlpha = 254; // Opacity = ai1/180 渐显(:46410)
368	    game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy - 80, '#FFE080', 20, 2.5);
369	  }
370	  const phase2 = e.ai3 === 1 || e.ai3 === 3; // AI_120_HallowBoss_IsInPhase2 :46179
371	  const b = baseOf(e);
372	  // :47296-47301 二阶段防御 ×1.2(ShouldEmpressBeEnraged 白天暴怒分支按任务不实现:白天不逃不暴怒)
373	  e.def.defense = Math.floor(phase2 ? b.def * 1.2 : b.def);
374	
375	  // 目标失效/超 6400px → 淡出退场(:46437-46444/:46595 num36=13;此后只有 13 态不触 player)
376	  if ((!player || player.dead || Math.hypot(player.cx - e.cx, player.cy - e.cy) > 6400) && e.ai0 !== 13) {
377	    e.ai0 = 13; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 += 1;
378	    e.vx /= 4; e.vy /= 4;
379	  }
380	  if (player) e.facing = player.cx > e.cx ? 1 : -1;
381	  if (!player) {
382	    // 目标彻底失效:上方已强制 ai0=13,此处推进淡出自毁(:47236/:47264-47271)
383	    e.spawnAlpha = Math.min(254, e.spawnAlpha + 5);
384	    e.vx *= 0.95; e.vy *= 0.95;
385	    e.ai1 += 1;
386	    flyIntegrate(e);
387	    if (e.ai1 >= 20 && e.spawnAlpha >= 254) e.dead = true;
388	    return;
389	  }
390	
391	  // 冲刺窗口伤害倍率(num16,:46352/:47006):8/9 态 40-90t 时 ×1.5
392	  const dashBoost = (e.ai0 === 8 || e.ai0 === 9) && e.ai1 > 40 && e.ai1 <= 90;
393	  e.def.damage = Math.round(b.dmg * (dashBoost ? 1.5 : 1));
394	
395	  switch (e.ai0) {
396	    // ---- 0 出生(:46370-46422):velocity=(0,5) 后 0.95 阻尼,180t 渐显;全程无敌(flag6=false) ----
397	    case 0: {
398	      if (e.ai1 === 0) { e.vx = 0; e.vy = 5; }
399	      e.iframes = 2; // :46408-46409 flag6=false → dontTakeDamage
400	      e.vx *= 0.95; e.vy *= 0.95;
401	      e.spawnAlpha = Math.max(0, 254 - Math.floor(clamp(e.ai1 / 180, 0, 1) * 254));
402	      e.ai1 += 1;
403	      if (e.ai1 >= 180) { e.ai0 = 1; e.ai1 = 0; }
404	      break;
405	    }
406	    // ---- 1 悬浮蓄力枢纽(:46418-46511):前 10t DashTo 冲近,之后阻尼;到时按表选招 ----
407	    case 1: {
408	      const dur = phase2 ? 20 : 45; // num34 :46419(expert/getGoodWorld 折半分支不实现)
409	      if (e.ai1 <= 10) empressDashTo(e, player.cx, player.cy);
410	      if (Math.hypot(e.vx, e.vy) > 16 && e.ai1 > 10) { e.vx /= 2; e.vy /= 2; }
411	      e.vx *= 0.92; e.vy *= 0.92;
412	      e.ai1 += 1;
413	      if (e.ai1 < dur) break;
414	      const table = phase2 ? EMPRESS_CYCLE_P2 : EMPRESS_CYCLE_P1;
415	      let next = table[Math.floor(e.ai2) % table.length];
416	      if (!phase2 && e.hp / e.maxHp <= 0.5) next = 10; // :46463 半血强制变身
417	      if (next === 8 && player.cx > e.cx) next = 9;     // :46493 冲刺方向
418	      e.ai0 = next; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 += 1;
419	      break;
420	    }
421	    // ---- 2 彩虹射线 873(:46512-46649):悬玩家左上(-150,-250),每 3t 一发直飞,60t 止 ----
422	    case 2: {
423	      empressHover(e, player, -150, -250);
424	      const dur = 60 + (90 - (phase2 ? 15 : 0)); // num90 - num17 :46517/:46356
425	      if (Math.floor(e.ai1) % 3 === 0 && e.ai1 < 60) {
426	        const [dvx, dvy] = rot(0, -6, (Math.PI / 2) * (Math.random() * 2 - 1));
427	        shoot(game, e.cx - 55, e.cy - 30, dvx, dvy, 45, 873);
428	      }
429	      e.ai1 += 1;
430	      if (e.ai1 >= dur) { e.ai0 = 1; e.ai1 = 0; }
431	      break;
432	    }
433	    // ---- 4 光枪墙 919(:46695-46779):悬玩家上 (0,-350),每 4t 在玩家周围 300px 环上布枪 ----
434	    case 4: {
435	      empressHover(e, player, 0, -350);
436	      const dur = 100 + (20 - (phase2 ? 15 : 0));
437	      // :46715 每 4t 布一杆;:46734 距玩家 >2400 跳过当拍(原版 continue,计时不断)
438	      if (Math.floor(e.ai1) % 4 === 0 && e.ai1 < 100 && Math.hypot(player.cx - e.cx, player.cy - e.cy) <= 2400) {
439	        const num82 = 4; // 经典枪数(expert 5)
440	        const num85 = Math.floor(e.ai1) / 4;
441	        let ux = Math.cos(Math.PI / (num82 * 2) + num85 * (Math.PI / num82));
442	        let uy = Math.sin(Math.PI / (num82 * 2) + num85 * (Math.PI / num82));
443	        ux += ux > 0 ? 0.5 : -0.5; // 经典横向外扩(:46725-46727)
444	        const ul = Math.hypot(ux, uy) || 1; ux /= ul; uy /= ul;
445	        if (player.vx * ux + player.vy * uy > 0) { ux = -ux; uy = -uy; } // 逆着玩家速度布枪(:46738)
446	        const leadX = player.cx + player.vx * 90, leadY = player.cy + player.vy * 90;
447	        let px = player.cx + ux * 300 - player.vx * 30;
448	        let py = player.cy + uy * 300 - player.vy * 30;
449	        const pd = Math.hypot(px - player.cx, py - player.cy);
450	        if (pd < 300) { // 环距不足外推(:46724-46731)
451	          const ex = player.cx - px, ey = player.cy - py;
452	          const el = Math.hypot(ex, ey) || 1;
453	          px = player.cx - (ex / el) * 300; py = player.cy - (ey / el) * 300;
454	        }
455	        const ax = leadX - px, ay = leadY - py;
456	        const al = Math.hypot(ax, ay) || 1;
457	        // 原版 919 零速预告后沿 v4 方向冲刺(Projectile.AI 收敛:12 速直飞)
458	        shoot(game, px, py, (ax / al) * 12, (ay / al) * 12, 50, 919, 240);
459	      }
460	      e.ai1 += 1;
461	      if (e.ai1 >= dur) { e.ai0 = 1; e.ai1 = 0; }
462	      break;
463	    }
464	    // ---- 5 永恒彩虹 872(:46780-46870):悬玩家上,开场 13 发环形彩弹(8 速) ----
465	    case 5: {
466	      empressHover(e, player, 0, -350);
467	      const dur = 42 + (30 - (phase2 ? 15 : 0));
468	      if (Math.floor(e.ai1) % 42 === 0 && e.ai1 < 42) {
469	        const base = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
470	        for (let k = 0; k < 13; k++) {
471	          const t = k / 13;
472	          const [ux, uy] = rot(0, 1, Math.PI / 2 + Math.PI * 2 * t + base);
473	          const [ox, oy] = rot(ux, uy, -Math.PI / 2);
474	          shoot(game, e.cx + 55 + ox * 30, e.cy - 30 + oy * 30, ux * 8, uy * 8, 45, 872);
475	        }
476	        game.playSfx('roar', 0.4);
477	      }
478	      e.ai1 += 1;
479	      if (e.ai1 >= dur) { e.ai0 = 1; e.ai1 = 0; }
480	      break;
481	    }
482	    // ---- 6 太阳舞 923(:46871-46935):悬玩家上 (-80,-500) 低速,每 60t 一环 6 发,3 环 ----
483	    case 6: {
484	      empressHover(e, player, -80, -500, 0.3, 0.7);
485	      const dur = 180 + (120 - (phase2 ? 15 : 0));
486	      if (Math.floor(e.ai1) % 60 === 0 && e.ai1 < 180) {
487	        const volley = Math.floor(e.ai1) / 60;
488	        const flip = player.cx > e.cx ? 1 : 0;
489	        for (let t = 0; t < 1; t += 1 / 6) {
490	          const slot = (t + (1 / 6) * 0.5 + volley * (1 / 6) * 0.5) % 1;
491	          const a = Math.PI * 2 * (slot + flip);
492	          // 原版 923 零速绕体公转(收敛:8 速放射)
493	          shoot(game, e.cx, e.cy - 100, Math.cos(a) * 8, Math.sin(a) * 8, 50, 923, 240);
494	        }
495	      }
496	      e.ai1 += 1;
497	      if (e.ai1 >= dur) { e.ai0 = 1; e.ai1 = 0; }
498	      break;
499	    }
500	    // ---- 7 光枪回环 919(二阶段,:46936-47049):每 60t 以玩家为中心布 13 连枪线,4 线 ----
501	    case 7: {
502	      empressHover(e, player, 0, -350, 0.4, 1);
503	      const dur = 240 + (20 - (phase2 ? 15 : 0));
504	      if (Math.floor(e.ai1) % 60 === 0 && e.ai1 < 240) {
505	        const line = Math.floor(e.ai1) / 60;
506	        let cx0 = player.cx, cy0 = player.cy;
507	        let dirX = 0, dirY = 0, axX = 0, axY = 0;
508	        const span = 1950; // num76 = 13*150 :46961
509	        switch (line) { // :46976-47011 四线几何
510	          case 0: cx0 += -span / 2; dirX = 0; dirY = span; axX = 1; axY = 0; break;
511	          case 1: cx0 += span / 2; cy0 += 75; dirX = 0; dirY = span; axX = -1; axY = 0; break;
512	          case 2: cx0 += -span * 0.4; cy0 += -span * 0.4; dirX = span * 1.4; dirY = 0; axX = 1; axY = 1; break;
513	          default: cx0 += span * 0.4 + 75; cy0 += -span * 0.4; dirX = -span * 1.4; dirY = 0; axX = -1; axY = 1; break;
514	        }
515	        const al = Math.hypot(axX, axY) || 1;
516	        for (let t = 0; t <= 1.0001; t += 1 / 13) {
517	          const ox = cx0 + dirX * (t - 0.5), oy = cy0 + dirY * (t - 0.5);
518	          shoot(game, ox, oy, (axX / al) * 12, (axY / al) * 12, 70, 919, 240);
519	        }
520	        game.playSfx('roar', 0.45);
521	      }
522	      e.ai1 += 1;
523	      if (e.ai1 >= dur) { e.ai0 = 1; e.ai1 = 0; }
524	      break;
525	    }
526	    // ---- 8/9 刀阵冲刺(:47053-47089):0-40t 冲到玩家侧后 550px(6-40t 无敌),
527	    //      40-90t 以 ±50 横速突进(伤害 ×1.5),其后 0.92 阻尼 ----
528	    case 8:
529	    case 9: {
530	      const side = e.ai0 === 8 ? -1 : 1; // num33 :47056
531	      if (e.ai1 <= 40) {
532	        if (e.ai1 >= 6) e.iframes = 2; // flag6 → dontTakeDamage(:47058)
533	        const destX = player.cx + side * -550, destY = player.cy;
534	        const dx = destX - e.cx, dy = destY - e.cy;
535	        const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
536	        simpleFly(e, (dx / d) * 12, (dy / d) * 12, 1.0);
537	        if (e.ai1 === 40) { e.vx *= 0.3; e.vy *= 0.3; }
538	      } else if (e.ai1 <= 90) {
539	        e.vx = vLerp(side * 50, e.vx, 0.05);
540	        e.vy = vLerp(0, e.vy, 0.05);
541	        if (e.ai1 === 90) { e.vx *= 0.7; e.vy *= 0.7; }
542	      } else {
543	        e.vx *= 0.92; e.vy *= 0.92;
544	      }
545	      e.ai1 += 1;
546	      if (e.ai1 >= 90 + (20 - (phase2 ? 15 : 0))) { e.ai0 = 1; e.ai1 = 0; }
547	      break;
548	    }
549	    // ---- 10 半血变身(:47106-47135):90t 时传送玩家上 250 处并 ai3 0→1(进入二阶段) ----
550	    case 10: {
551	      if (e.ai1 >= 30 && e.ai1 <= 170) e.iframes = 2; // flag6 → dontTakeDamage(:47110)
552	      e.vx *= 0.95; e.vy *= 0.95;
553	      if (e.ai1 === 90) {
554	        if (e.ai3 === 0) e.ai3 = 1; // ai3==2(白天暴怒)分支不实现
555	        e.x = player.cx - e.w / 2;
556	        e.y = player.cy - 250 - e.h / 2;
557	        game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy, '#FFEE90', 24, 3);
558	        game.playSfx('roar', 0.8);
559	      }
560	      e.ai1 += 1;
561	      if (e.ai1 >= 180 + (20 - (phase2 ? 15 : 0))) { e.ai0 = 1; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; }
562	      break;
563	    }
564	    // ---- 13 淡出退场(:47226-47281):alpha 渐隐,全隐后移除 ----
565	    case 13: {
566	      e.spawnAlpha = Math.min(254, e.spawnAlpha + 5);
567	      e.ai1 += 1;
568	      if (e.ai1 >= 20 && e.spawnAlpha >= 254) e.dead = true;
569	      break;
570	    }
571	    default:
572	      e.ai0 = 1; e.ai1 = 0;
573	      break;
574	  }
575	  // :47292 alpha 渐隐回落(0/13 态各自管控 spawnAlpha,不参与)
576	  if (e.ai0 !== 0 && e.ai0 !== 13) e.spawnAlpha = Math.max(0, e.spawnAlpha - 5);
577	  flyIntegrate(e);
578	}
579	
580	// ===================== AI_121 史莱姆皇后 657(NPC.cs:45543-46177) =====================
581	// 状态机(ai0): 0 待机(一阶段地面/二阶段 FlyMovement) / 1 传送后显形 / 2 传送消散 /
582	//   3 走路连跳(-8,-8,-6,-13 四连) / 4 升空砸地(ai2=0 升空 60t → ai2=1 重力坠,落地撒 922) /
583	//   5 凝胶环(ai2=0 蓄 50t → ai2=1 撒 926 环)。
584	// 半血(flag2)进二阶段:悬浮 FlyMovement + 攻速变化 + 暴怒(离开神圣之地)分支按任务跳过。
585	// 经典数值(:45546-45552): 砸地 922 40 伤 / 凝胶环 926 30 伤;砸地蓄力 30t(二阶段 10t);
586	//   凝胶环 6 发(二阶段 10 发);仆从按掉血 2%(二阶段 1.5%)撒 1-2 只 NPC 658/659/660。
587	const QUEEN_MINION_VANILLA = [658, 659, 660]; // vanilla :46134-46146 蓝/粉/紫仆从(1.4.0.5 与 1.4.5.6 同 id)
588	// 658-660 未入 vanilla-npcs.json(提取遗漏)时的兜底:全部取真实史莱姆族——
589	// 任务清单的 535/536/537 实为 SlimeSpiked/TheBride(僵尸!)/SandSlime(NPCID.cs:12135-12139),
590	// 536 不可用,故以 16 蓝史莱姆补位
591	const QUEEN_MINION_FALLBACK = [535, 537, 16];
592	
593	/** AI_015_KingSlime_FindTeleportSpot(NPC.cs:43402-43434)收敛:anti → 玩家脚下;
594	 *  否则玩家 10 格半径内找"脚下实心 + 头顶 7 格开阔"的随机格,Bottom 落点返回(:43417-43418) */
595	function queenTeleportSpot(game: GameHooks, player: Player, anti: boolean): { x: number; y: number; found: boolean } {
596	  if (anti) return { x: player.x + player.w / 2, y: player.y + player.h, found: false }; // :43406-43409
597	  const st = game.world.store;
598	  const ptx = Math.floor(player.cx / TILE), pty = Math.floor(player.cy / TILE);
599	  const cands: Array<[number, number]> = [];
600	  for (let x = ptx - 10; x <= ptx + 10; x++) {
601	    for (let y = pty - 10; y <= pty + 10; y++) {
602	      if (!st.inBounds(x, y) || !st.isSolid(x, y)) continue;
603	      let clear = true;
604	      for (let k = 1; k <= 7; k++) {                       // 头顶 7 格(BuildCache 10/7 参数)
605	        if (st.inBounds(x, y - k) && st.isSolid(x, y - k)) { clear = false; break; }
606	      }
607	      if (clear) cands.push([x, y]);
608	    }
609	  }
610	  if (cands.length > 0) {
611	    const [x, y] = cands[Math.floor(Math.random() * cands.length)];
612	    return { x: x * TILE + TILE / 2, y: y * TILE, found: true };
613	  }
614	  return { x: player.x + player.w / 2, y: player.y + player.h, found: false }; // :43430-43433 兜底
615	}
616	
617	/** AI_121_QueenSlime_FlyMovement(:45467-45542):12 速/0.085 加速度趋近玩家上方 250,
618	 *  反向且 >100px 时加速度 ×2;旋转 = vx*0.1 钳 ±0.5 */
619	function queenFlyMovement(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player): void {
620	  e.onGround = false;
621	  const spd = 12, accBase = 0.085, hover = 250;
622	  let tx = player.cx, ty = player.cy - hover;
623	  if (losClear(game, e.cx, e.cy, player.cx, player.cy)) {
624	    tx = player.cx; ty = player.cy - hover;
625	  } else {
626	    // :45478-45496 无视线 → 逐级上探玩家上方第一个实心格下方(简化:玩家上方 16px 一档,探 16 档)
627	    const st = game.world.store;
628	    let found = false;
629	    for (let i = 0; i < 16; i++) {
630	      const py = player.cy - 16 * i;
631	      const ptx = Math.floor(player.cx / TILE), pty = Math.floor(py / TILE);
632	      if (st.inBounds(ptx, pty) && st.isSolid(ptx, pty)) {
633	        tx = player.cx; ty = py + 16;
634	        found = true;
635	        break;
636	      }
637	    }
638	    if (!found) { tx = player.cx; ty = player.cy; }
639	  }
640	  let dvx = tx - e.cx, dvy = ty - e.cy;
641	  const dist = Math.hypot(dvx, dvy);
642	  let acc = accBase;
643	  if (Math.abs(dvx) < 40) dvx = e.vx;                       // :45512-45514
644	  const lim = spd - 2;
645	  if (dist > 100 && ((e.vx < -lim && dvx > 0) || (e.vx > lim && dvx < 0))) acc *= 2; // :45516-45519
646	  if (dist < 40) { dvx = e.vx; dvy = e.vy; }                // :45521-45522
647	  else if (dist < 80) { dvx = (dvx / (dist || 1)) * spd * 0.65; dvy = (dvy / (dist || 1)) * spd * 0.65; }
648	  else { dvx = (dvx / (dist || 1)) * spd; dvy = (dvy / (dist || 1)) * spd; }
649	  simpleFly(e, dvx, dvy, acc);
650	  e.spin = clamp(e.vx * 0.1, -0.5, 0.5); // :45536-45541
651	}
652	
653	export function queenSlimeAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null): void {
654	  const la = laOf(e);
655	  if (!e.bInit) {
656	    // :45559-45566 localAI[0]==0:ai[1]=-100,localAI[0]=lifeMax
657	    e.bInit = true;
658	    e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = -100; e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 = 0;
659	    la.la0 = e.maxHp;
660	  }
661	  const phase2 = e.hp <= e.maxHp / 2; // flag2 :45552
662	  // 掉血阈值撒仆从(:46147-46177):localAI[0] 记上次标记血量,差值超 2%(二阶段 1.5%)即撒
663	  if (la.la0 >= e.maxHp / 2 && e.hp < e.maxHp / 2) {
664	    la.la0 = e.hp;            // :46142-46148 进二阶段复位状态
665	    e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0;
666	  }
667	  const b = baseOf(e);
668	  e.def.damage = b.dmg;
669	  e.def.defense = b.def;
670	  // 玩家死/超 500 格 → 鼓励离场(EncourageDespawn:45576;简化为远距上浮,不清除)
671	  if (!player || player.dead || Math.abs(e.cx - player.cx) / 16 > 500) {
672	    e.encourageDespawn(10); // :45574 二次判定后仍失败 → EncourageDespawn(10)
673	    if (player) e.facing = player.cx < e.cx ? 1 : -1; // 背对玩家（:45576-45582）
674	    e.vy -= 0.06;
675	    e.y += e.vy; e.x += e.vx;
676	    e.spin = clamp(e.vx * 0.1, -0.5, 0.5);
677	    return;
678	  }
679	  const dirToPlayer = player.cx >= e.cx ? 1 : -1;
680	  e.facing = dirToPlayer;
681	
682	  // 传送蓄能(ai3,一阶段专属 :45576-45618):无视线或高差 >320 时 +1.5/t,否则 -1/t;
683	  // 满 300 且落地待机 → 进传送(2)。落点 = AI_015_KingSlime_FindTeleportSpot(NPC.cs:43402)
684	  // 收敛:玩家 10 格半径内"脚下实心+头顶 7 格开阔"的随机格(:43412 BuildCache 10/7 参数);
685	  // 反挂机(蓄满 360 或距玩家 >2000)→ 直接落到玩家脚下(:43404-43410)。
686	  if (!phase2) {
687	    const blocked = !losClear(game, e.cx, e.cy, player.cx, player.cy) || Math.abs(e.y - (player.y + player.h)) > 320;
688	    if (blocked) e.ai3 += 1.5;
689	    else e.ai3 = Math.max(0, e.ai3 - 1);
690	    if (e.ai3 >= 300 && e.ai0 === 0 && e.vy === 0 && e.onGround) {
691	      const anti = e.ai3 >= 360 || Math.hypot(player.cx - e.cx, player.cy - e.cy) > 2000; // :45588-45595
692	      if (e.ai3 >= 360) e.ai3 = 360;      // :45591-45593
693	      const spot = queenTeleportSpot(game, player, anti);
694	      la.la1 = spot.x; la.la2 = spot.y;
695	      if (anti || spot.found) { e.ai0 = 2; e.ai1 = 0; }
696	      else e.ai3 = 0;                     // :45596-45599 找不到落点 → 重攒
697	    }
698	  }
699	
700	  // noGravity/noTileCollide 置位(:45634-45635 默认关;case 4/5 与二阶段飞行开启)——
701	  // 原版 case 4 整段(含 ai2=0 升空)置 noGravity,故一阶段升空同样免重力
702	  const flying = e.ai0 === 5 || (e.ai0 === 4 && e.ai2 === 0) || (phase2 && e.ai0 === 0);
703	  if (!flying) {
704	    // 通用重力(NPC.cs:91519-91524,!noGravity 时 +0.3 钳 maxFallSpeed 10;
705	    // 俯冲段(:45945)自身 +1 钳 14 与此叠加,实际仍受 10 上限——与原版一致)
706	    if (!e.onGround) e.vy += 0.3;
707	    if (e.vy > 10) e.vy = 10;
708	  } else {
709	    e.onGround = false;
710	  }
711	
712	  switch (e.ai0) {
713	    // ---- 0 待机(:45628-45686):地面摩擦 / 二阶段飞行;ai1 过阈值(60/120)选下一招 ----
714	    case 0: {
715	      if (phase2) {
716	        queenFlyMovement(e, game, player);
717	      } else if (e.onGround) {
718	        e.vx *= 0.8;
719	        if (Math.abs(e.vx) < 0.1) e.vx = 0;
720	        e.spin = 0;
721	      }
722	      // :45673 一阶段空中(!flag2 && velocity.Y!=0)不计数,落地后才推进选拍
723	      if (!phase2 && !e.onGround && e.vy !== 0) break;
724	      e.ai1 += 1;
725	      const idle = phase2 ? 120 : 60; // num10 :45648-45652
726	      if (e.ai1 > idle) {
727	        e.ai1 = 0;
728	        if (phase2) {
729	          // :45660-45678 二阶段:默认砸地(ai2=1),玩家在上方或横距 >250 改凝胶环
730	          let next = Math.random() < 0.5 ? 4 : 5;
731	          let sub = next === 4 ? 1 : 0;
732	          if (next === 4 && (player.y + player.h < e.y + e.h || Math.abs(player.cx - e.cx) > 250)) {
733	            next = 5; sub = 0;
734	          }
735	          e.ai0 = next; e.ai2 = sub;
736	        } else {
737	          // :45680-45686 一阶段:随机 跳(3)/砸地(4)/凝胶环(5)
738	          const r = Math.floor(Math.random() * 3);
739	          e.ai0 = r === 0 ? 3 : r === 1 ? 4 : 5;
740	          e.ai2 = 0;
741	        }
742	      }
743	      break;
744	    }
745	    // ---- 1 传送后显形(:45689-45720):30t;缩放动画(num3)为纯视觉,跳过 ----
746	    case 1: {
747	      e.spin = 0;
748	      game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy, '#7040A0', 2, 1.2);
749	      e.ai1 += 1;
750	      if (e.ai1 >= 30) { e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; }
751	      break;
752	    }
753	    // ---- 2 传送消散(:45721-45758):60t 后落位到 la1/la2(底部对齐) ----
754	    case 2: {
755	      e.spin = 0;
756	      e.ai1 += 1;
757	      if (e.ai1 >= 60 && la.la1 !== 0) {
758	        e.iframes = 2; // dontTakeDamage = hide(:46138)
759	        e.x = la.la1 - e.w / 2;
760	        e.y = la.la2 - e.h;
761	        e.vx = 0; e.vy = 0;
762	        e.ai0 = 1; e.ai1 = 0;
763	        game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy, '#7040A0', 16, 2.4);
764	      } else if (e.ai1 > 90) {
765	        e.ai0 = 1; e.ai1 = 0; // 找不到落点的兜底
766	      }
767	      break;
768	    }
769	    // ---- 3 走路连跳(:45759-45844):四连跳 vy=-8/-8/-6/-13,横速递增 4/4.5/4/3.5;
770	    //      落地重跳倒计时 ai1(血量 <66%/<33% 各 +4/t 加速) ----
771	    case 3: {
772	      e.spin = 0;
773	      if (e.onGround) {
774	        e.vx *= 0.8;
775	        if (Math.abs(e.vx) < 0.1) e.vx = 0;
776	        e.ai1 += 4;
777	        if (e.hp < e.maxHp * 0.66) e.ai1 += 4;
778	        if (e.hp < e.maxHp * 0.33) e.ai1 += 4;
779	        if (e.ai1 >= 0) {
780	          if (e.ai2 === 3) {
781	            e.vy = -13; e.vx += 3.5 * dirToPlayer;
782	            e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0;
783	            e.ai0 = 0; // 大跳结束回待机
784	            game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.y + e.h, '#8060C0', 10, 2.2);
785	          } else if (e.ai2 === 2) {
786	            e.vy = -6; e.vx += 4.5 * dirToPlayer;
787	            e.ai1 = -40; e.ai2 += 1;
788	          } else {
789	            e.vy = -8; e.vx += 4 * dirToPlayer;
790	            e.ai1 = -40; e.ai2 += 1;
791	          }
792	          e.onGround = false;
793	        }
794	      } else {
795	        // 空中横移(:45819-45844):朝玩家 0.2/t,上限 3(反向时 0.93 阻尼)
796	        const cap = 3;
797	        if ((dirToPlayer === 1 && e.vx < cap) || (dirToPlayer === -1 && e.vx > -cap)) {
798	          if ((dirToPlayer === -1 && e.vx < 0.1) || (dirToPlayer === 1 && e.vx > -0.1)) {
799	            e.vx += 0.2 * dirToPlayer;
800	          } else {
801	            e.vx *= 0.93;
802	          }
803	        }
804	      }
805	      break;
806	    }
807	    // ---- 4 升空砸地(:45845-46006):ai2=0 飞向玩家上 384(60t) → ai2=1 重力坠(蓄力 30/10t,
808	    //      vy+1/t 钳 14);落地撒 922 冲击波(40 伤)+ 尘暴,回待机 ----
809	    case 4: {
810	      e.spin *= 0.9;
811	      if (e.ai2 === 1) {
812	        const num13 = phase2 ? 10 : 30; // :45856-45860
813	        e.vx *= 0.8;
814	        if (!e.onGround) {
815	          const prev = e.ai1;
816	          e.ai1 += 1;
817	          if (e.ai1 >= num13) {
818	            if (prev < num13) game.playSfx('roar', 0.5);
819	            e.vy += 1;
820	            if (e.vy === 0) e.vy = 0.01;
821	            if (e.vy >= 14) e.vy = 14; // num17 = 14(经典;getGoodWorld 15.99 不实现)
822	          } else {
823	            e.vy *= 0.8;
824	          }
825	          if (phase2 && e.ai1 > num13 + 120) { // 二阶段超时兜底(:45931-45938)

976	    }

// 弹跳球族增量（2026-08-11）：665 ChaosBallTim / 666 VileSpitEaterOfWorlds 两条取自
// Terarria1456/NPC.cs SetDefaults :17338-17367（aiStyle 9，字段对齐 30/112 同族；贴图
// NPC_665/666.png 已在 sprites，此前纯数据缺口）。
// 星璇塔增量（2026-08-11）：403/404（星尘蠕虫身/尾，字段复制 402 改宽高——1456 无独立
// SetDefaults，原版由 DrawNPC case 402 自绘 trail；本仓蠕虫链 spawnWormChain 需要
// 头+1/头+2 条目）、408（小水流入侵者，复制 407 缩小——1456 无独立 SetDefaults）。
// aiStyle 与 AI_XXX 方法的对应见 NPC.cs AI() 分发；此处先覆盖高频家族，
// 未移植 AI 家族的 aiStyle 回退到最接近的已移植行为（注释标注）。
import vanillaNpcsJson from '../../public/sprites/vanilla-npcs.json';
import vanillaDataJson from '../../public/sprites/vanilla.json';
import { ITEM_BY_KEY } from './items';
import { TILE_DEFS } from './tiles';

/** tile id → key 反查（biomeAt 群系判定用） */
const TILE_KEY_NAME: string[] = TILE_DEFS.map((d) => d.key);

// （旧掉落表已退役：掉落走 src/drops/NpcDrops.ts 规则树——
//  tools/extract-npcdrops.mjs 结构化提取 ItemDropDatabase.cs，含专家/大师/条件/链语义）

/** 原版物品 id → 本仓库 item key（vanilla.json 的 key 是 PascalCase，ITEM_BY_KEY 多为 snake_case；
 *  未注册的返回 null 跳过） */
export const vanillaItemKey = (() => {
  const map = new Map<number, string | null>();
  const items = (vanillaDataJson as unknown as { items: Record<string, { key?: string }> }).items ?? {};
  return (itemId: number): string | null => {
    if (map.has(itemId)) return map.get(itemId)!;
    const meta = items[String(itemId)];
    let key: string | null = null;
    if (meta?.key) {
      const snake = meta.key.replace(/([a-z0-9])([A-Z])/g, '$1_$2').toLowerCase();
      if (ITEM_BY_KEY[snake] != null) key = snake;
      else if (ITEM_BY_KEY[meta.key] != null) key = meta.key;
      // vi_NNN 导入物品（items.ts 的全量原版物品命名）：vi_<id> 或 vi_<id>_<snake>
      else if (ITEM_BY_KEY[`vi_${itemId}`] != null) key = `vi_${itemId}`;
      else if (ITEM_BY_KEY[`vi_${itemId}_${snake}`] != null) key = `vi_${itemId}_${snake}`;
    }
    map.set(itemId, key);

```

904	    if (p && !p.dead && segHitsRect(this.cx, this.cy, dirX * this.len, dirY * this.len, wSq, p)) {
905	      game.damagePlayer(this.dmg, this.cx, this.cy, src);
906	    }
907	  }
908	
909	  /** Collision.LaserScan 近似:垂直束向 ±半宽 3 条采样线,8px 步进至首实心块 */
910	  private laserScan(game: GameHooks, dirX: number, dirY: number, width: number): number {
911	    const px = -dirY, py = dirX; // 垂直束向
912	    let sum = 0;
913	    for (let k = -1; k <= 1; k++) {
914	      const off = (k * width) / 2;
915	      const ox = this.cx + px * off, oy = this.cy + py * off;
916	      const steps = Math.ceil(2400 / 8);
917	      let d = 2400;
918	      for (let i = 1; i <= steps; i++) {
919	        const t = i * 8;
920	        const tx = Math.floor((ox + dirX * t) / TILE), ty = Math.floor((oy + dirY * t) / TILE);
921	        const st = game.world.store;
922	        if (!st.inBounds(tx, ty)) { d = t - 8; break; }
923	        if (st.isSolid(tx, ty)) { d = t - 8; break; }
924	      }
925	      sum += d;
926	    }
927	    return sum / 3;
928	  }
929	
930	  draw(r: Renderer, _cam: Camera): void {
931	    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');
932	    if (!ctx) return;
933	    const scale = Math.min(this.maxScale, Math.sin((this.age * Math.PI) / 180) * 10 * this.maxScale);
934	    if (scale <= 0.01 || this.len <= 1) return;
935	    const thick = Math.max(2, 36 * scale);
936	    ctx.save();
937	    ctx.globalAlpha = clamp(scale * 1.6, 0.25, 1);
938	    ctx.translate(this.cx, this.cy);
939	    ctx.rotate(this.angle);
940	    ctx.fillStyle = '#B040D0';
941	    ctx.fillRect(0, -thick / 2, this.len, thick);
942	    ctx.globalAlpha = clamp(scale * 0.9, 0.15, 0.6);
943	    ctx.fillStyle = '#F0C8FF';
944	    ctx.fillRect(0, -thick / 4, this.len, thick / 2);
945	    ctx.restore();
946	  }
947	}

949	/** 线段 vs AABB(原版 Collision.CheckAABBvLineCollision 语义:线宽胶囊与矩形相交) */
950	function segHitsRect(x0: number, y0: number, dx: number, dy: number, width: number,
951	  t: { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number }): boolean {
952	  // 最近点参数 t∈[0,1](钳到段内)
953	  const cx = t.x + t.w / 2, cy = t.y + t.h / 2;
954	  const len2 = dx * dx + dy * dy || 1;
955	  let k = ((cx - x0) * dx + (cy - y0) * dy) / len2;
956	  k = clamp(k, 0, 1);
957	  const px = x0 + dx * k, py = y0 + dy * k;
958	  // 最近点到矩形距离(逐轴钳位)
959	  const nx = clamp(px, t.x, t.x + t.w), ny = clamp(py, t.y, t.y + t.h);
960	  return Math.hypot(px - nx, py - ny) < width / 2;
961	}
962	
963	/** 死亡之光发射出口(原版 :37902-37916 头 / :38625-38637 真眼):
964	 *  起角 = 朝玩家角 ∓ 60°(num1278 = 玩家在左 +1),角速度 = num1278·2π/540。
965	 *  返回源 NPC 的扫射编码角初值((起角+3π)·num1278,头部 ai2 逐 t 解码步进用)。 */
966	function mlDeathray(game: GameHooks, src: Enemy, kind: 'head' | 'core', player: Player, dmg: number): number {
967	  const dx = player.cx - src.cx, dy = player.cy - src.cy;
968	  const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
969	  const n = dx / d < 0 ? 1 : -1;                       // num1278
970	  const ang = Math.atan2(dy, dx) - n * (TAU / 6);
971	  addProj(game, new MLDeathray(src, kind, ang, n * (TAU / 540), dmg));
972	  return (ang + Math.PI * 3) * n;
973	}

976	function mlCoreHover(e: Enemy, player: Player) {

981	    flyLerp(e, ((dx - e.vx) / d) * 8, ((dy - e.vy) / d) * 8, 0.5);
982	  }
983	}

1269	function handApproach(e: Enemy, tx: number, ty: number, spd: number, k: number) {

1272	  if (d < 0.01) return;
1273	  const m = Math.min(spd, d);

1275	  e.vy = vSmoothStep(e.vy, (dy / d) * m, k);
1276	}

  [0, 90], [1, 90], [0, 90], [2, 135], [0, 90], [3, 200], [0, 90], [4, 375], [0, 90], [2, 135],

export function moonLordFreeEyeAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
  if (rnd(420) === 0) game.playSfx('roar', 0.15, e.cx, e.cy);   // :38227 SoundID 29(100-101)
  // ---- 核心锚定(:38231-38237):ai[3]=核心 id,亡→静默消散 ----
  const core = enemies(game).find((n) => n.id === e.ai3 && n.vanillaId === 398);

  // ---- 攻击表推进(:38240-38264) ----

    }
  } else if (e.ai0 === 1) {

export function moonLeechAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, _player: Player | null) {
  const x = lunarLocalAI(e);
  if (!e.bInit) {
    e.bInit = true;
    e.ai2 = 0;
    x[0] = e.cx; x[1] = e.cy;    // 源弹 456 弹着点(=玩家处)近似:出生点即弹着点(:37832)
  }
  // ---- 头锚定(:38676-38684) ----
  const head = enemies(game).find((n) => n.id === Math.floor(e.ai0) - 1 && n.vanillaId === 396);
  if (!head || head.dead) { e.dead = true; return; }
  e.ai2 += 1;
  const LEECH_T = 90;                                       // num1321
  if (e.ai2 >= LEECH_T) {
    // ---- 治疗回灌(:38689-38772):池 1000,按 头→核心→手0→手1 亏血序 ----
    const core = enemies(game).find((n) => n.id === head.ai3 && n.vanillaId === 398);
    const hands = enemies(game).filter((n) => !n.dead && n.vanillaId === 397 && n.ai3 === head.ai3);
    const hand0 = hands.find((n) => n.ai2 === 0);
    const hand1 = hands.find((n) => n.ai2 === 1);
    let pool = 1000;                                        // num1329
    for (const tgt of [head, core, hand0, hand1]) {
      if (!tgt || tgt.dead || pool <= 0) continue;
      const deficit = tgt.maxHp - tgt.hp;
      if (deficit <= 0) continue;
      const amt = Math.min(pool, deficit);                  // num1335 = pool+min(deficit-pool,0)
      tgt.hp += amt;
      pool -= amt;
      game.spawnParticles(tgt.cx, tgt.cy, '#5AE86A',
        Math.min(8, 2 + Math.floor(amt / 100)), 1.6, { life: 30 });   // HealEffect 近似
    }
    e.dead = true;                                          // :38770-38772 静默消散
    return;
  }
  // ---- 插值飞行(:38775-38791):出生点 → 头额 ----
  e.vx = 0; e.vy = 0;
  const k = e.ai2 / LEECH_T;
  e.x = lerp(x[0], head.cx, k) - e.w / 2;
  e.y = lerp(x[1], head.cy + 216, k) - e.h / 2;
  game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy - e.h / 2, '#B040D0', 3, 0.7, { life: 20 });   // 紫尘双环
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// AI_083a 神秘石碑 437(NPC.cs:38795-38920 + CultistRitual.cs CheckFloor)

/** CultistRitual.CheckFloor(CultistRitual.cs:88-124 1:1):i∈{-5,-3,3,5} → x=tileX+i*2,
 *  y 自 tileY-5 向下扫至 +11,取首个"实心/平台 且上方 3 格净空"的点;不足 4 点 → null */
function ritualCheckFloor(game: GameHooks, cx: number, cy: number): Array<{ x: number; y: number }> | null {
  const pts: Array<{ x: number; y: number }> = [];
  const tx = Math.floor(cx / TILE), ty = Math.floor(cy / TILE);
  for (const i of [-5, -3, 3, 5]) {
    const x = tx + i * 2;
    for (let j = -5; j < 12; j++) {
      const y = ty + j;
      if (y < 1 || y >= game.world.store.h - 1) break;
      const floor = solidTileAt(game, x, y) || game.world.store.isPlatform(x, y);
      if (!floor) continue;
      // 净空:本柱 3 格全空,或(本柱空 且 左右柱上两行空)——原版双条件或语义
      const clearMid = !solidRect(game, x - 1, x + 1, y - 3, y - 1);
      const clearSide = !solidRect(game, x, x, y - 3, y - 1)
        && !solidRect(game, x + 1, x + 1, y - 3, y - 2)
        && !solidRect(game, x - 1, x - 1, y - 3, y - 2);
      if (clearMid || clearSide) { pts.push({ x, y }); break; }
    }
  }
  return pts.length === 4 ? pts : null;
}

        Object.keys(this.vanilla.walls).map(Number),

  }
  /** 按需加载 vanilla 单图(去重;失败静默)。命中返回元素,否则 null。
   *  渲染路径一律用此入口(经 vimg 别名)——直接 vimages.get 会绕过懒加载与
   *  onVImageLoaded 重烘焙钩子(棕榈树干传送消失的根因) */
  ensureVImage(file: string): HTMLImageElement | null {
    const hit = this.vimages.get(file);
    if (hit) return hit;
    if (this._iconPending.has(file)) return null;
    this._iconPending.add(file);
    const im = new Image();
    im.onload = () => {
      this.vimages.set(file, im);
      this._iconPending.delete(file);
      this.onVImageLoaded?.(file);  // 晚到的表 → ChunkCache 重烘焙
    };
    im.onerror = () => { this._iconPending.delete(file); };
    im.src = `sprites/${encodeURI(file)}`;
    return null;
  }
  /** 后台预取全部物品图标(保留兼容入口;预载流程请用 awaited 的 preloadIcons) */
  prefetchIcons(): void {
    void this.preloadIcons();
  }

  private _iconsPromise: Promise<void> | null = null;
  /** 预载全部物品图标素材。图集化后清单 = 去重后的 ~3 张 Item_Atlas_k.png
   *  (此前 6059 张单体逐张请求);缓存 Promise——并发 await 的调用者
   *  都会等到同一批加载完成(此前旗标早退会让第二个调用者拿到假完成) */
  preloadIcons(onProgress?: (p: number) => void): Promise<void> {
    if (this._iconsPromise) return this._iconsPromise;
    const icons = [...new Set(Object.values(this.vanilla.items).map((m) => m.icon))];
    let done = 0;
    this._iconsPromise = Promise.all(icons.map((f) => new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
      if (this.vimages.has(f)) { done++; onProgress?.(done / icons.length); return resolve(); }
      const im = new Image();
      im.onload = () => { this.vimages.set(f, im); done++; onProgress?.(done / icons.length); resolve(); };
      im.onerror = () => resolve();

// ProjectileID.cs 对照：264 RainCloud 雨滴 / 435 MartianTurretBlast(特斯拉电球) /
// 438 MuonBlast(骑手弹) / 447 MartianDeathray(死光电报,自带 proj AI 未实装→直落代) /
// 448 MartianSaucerCannon(火炮弹) / 449 MartianSaucerLaser(激光) / 450 MartianMissile。
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
const RENDER_PROXY: Record<number, number> = {
  264: 118,   // 雨 → IceBolt
  435: 173,   // 特斯拉电球 → EnchantedBeam
  438: 173,   // 骑手弹 → EnchantedBeam
  447: 173,   // 死光电报 → EnchantedBeam
  448: 21,    // 火炮弹 → Bone(尖刺球)
  449: 173,   // 激光 → EnchantedBeam
  450: 102,   // 发射器导弹 → BombSkeletronPrime(火箭)
};

function shootM(
  game: GameHooks, x: number, y: number, vx: number, vy: number,
  dmg: number, projId: number, life = 300, pierce = 1,
): void {
  const rid = RENDER_PROXY[projId] ?? projId;
  (game as unknown as { entities: { add: (e: unknown, b: string) => void } }).entities.add(
    new Arrow(x, y, vx, vy, dmg, 0, rid, null, { grav: 0, life, pierce }),
    'projectiles');
}

/** 脚下净空扫描（cs:36400-36421 同构）：返回到第一个实心面的像素距离。
 *  脚下即实心 → 16px（原版 num=1）；150t 内无面 → 150*16 */
function groundGapPx(game: GameHooks, e: Enemy): number {
  const st = game.world.store;
  const tx = Math.floor(e.cx / TILE);
  const ty = Math.floor((e.y + e.h) / TILE);
  const solidAt = (x: number, y: number) =>
    x >= 0 && y >= 0 && x < st.w && y < st.h && st.isSolid(x, y) && !st.half[st.idx(x, y)];
  if (solidAt(tx, ty)) return TILE;
  let n = 0;
  while (n < 150 && ty + n < st.h) {
    if (solidAt(tx, ty + n)) { n--; break; }
    n++;
  }
  return n * TILE;
}

/** 原版 CS 直积分（noTileCollide 族） */
const fly = (e: Enemy): void => { e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy; };
/** 单格实心（世界边界外视为空,飞碟出界由本 AI 兜底） */
function solidTile(game: GameHooks, x: number, y: number): boolean {
  const st = game.world.store;
  const tx = Math.floor(x / TILE), ty = Math.floor(y / TILE);
  return tx >= 0 && ty >= 0 && tx < st.w && ty < st.h && st.isSolid(tx, ty);
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// AI_080 火星探测器 399（NPC.cs:38129-38223）
// ai[0] 0=巡逻(3px/s 朝向走,贴地 15t 内上升) / 1=受惊悬停 60t(×0.95) /

// 箭矢/通用直飞·抛物线投射物（原版 Projectile type 1/2、aiStyle 1/2 主干，Projectile.cs:546-566 + AI_001/AI_002）：
// 重力 0.3/tick（aiStyle1/2 通用常量；直飞弹传 0）、timeLeft 1200、旋转 atan2(vy,vx)+π/2（AI_001 尾部 L54877）、
// 原版贴图 Projectile_N.png；命中敌人伤害/击退/暴击（穿透>1 时同敌免疫防连击）；
// 命中 tileCut 砍草/碎罐（Projectile.CutTiles）；命中实心块 1/3 概率回收掉落。
import { Entity } from './Entity';
import { hitCritters, playEnemyHitSound } from './projTargets';
import { TILE } from '../core/constants';
import type { GameHooks } from './types';
import type { Renderer } from '../render/Renderer';
import type { Camera } from '../render/Camera';

/** 原版投射物贴图懒加载（Projectile_1.png = 木箭、Projectile_2.png = 燃烧箭，泛用所有 id） */
const spriteCache = new Map<number, HTMLImageElement>();
export function projSprite(projId: number): HTMLImageElement | null {
  let img = spriteCache.get(projId);
  if (img !== undefined) return img ?? null;
  if (typeof Image === 'undefined') return null;
  img = new Image();
  img.src = `sprites/vanilla/Projectile_${projId}.png`;
  spriteCache.set(projId, img);
  return img;
}

export interface ArrowOpts {
  /** 重力/tick（aiStyle1/2 = 0.3；直飞魔法弹传 0）。默认 0.3 */
  grav?: number;
  /** 原版 timeLeft（Projectile.cs:554 默认 1200） */
  life?: number;
  /** 穿透次数（原版 penetrate：手里剑 4、箭 1；-1 视作 1） */
  pierce?: number;
}

export class Arrow extends Entity {
  w = 10; h = 10; // 原版 SetDefaults type 1：width/height = 10
  vx: number;
  vy: number;
  damage: number;
  knockback: number;
  /** 原版投射物类型（1=木箭 2=燃烧箭，PickAmmo projToShoot = ammo.shoot） */
  projId: number;
  /** 回收掉落的 item key（null = 不回收，如燃烧箭） */
  dropKey: string | null;
  grav: number;
  life: number;
  pierce: number;
  /** 穿透投射物的同敌免疫表（敌人 id 集合） */

  dead = false;

  constructor(x: number, y: number, vx: number, vy: number, damage: number,
    knockback: number, projId = 1, dropKey: string | null = null, opts?: ArrowOpts) {
    super();
    this.x = x; this.y = y;
    this.vx = vx; this.vy = vy;
    this.damage = damage;
    this.knockback = knockback;
    this.projId = projId;
    this.dropKey = dropKey;
    this.grav = opts?.grav ?? 0.3;
    this.life = opts?.life ?? 1200;
    this.pierce = opts?.pierce ?? 1;
  }

  draw(r: Renderer, _cam: Camera): void {
    // 世界坐标绘制(Renderer 实体循环运行在世界变换内,勿再自算屏幕坐标——
    // 曾双变换把箭甩出屏幕,表现为"箭隐形但能命中")
    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');
    if (!ctx) return;
    const ang = Math.atan2(this.vy, this.vx);
    const img = projSprite(this.projId);
    ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(this.x + this.w / 2, this.y + this.h / 2);
    ctx.rotate(ang + Math.PI / 2); // 原版贴图纵向：rotation = atan2+π/2（AI_001 L54877）
    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
    if (img && img.complete && img.naturalWidth > 0) {
      ctx.drawImage(img, -this.w / 2, -this.w / 2, this.w, this.w * (img.naturalHeight / img.naturalWidth));
    } else {
      // 贴图未就绪：短线兜底
      ctx.strokeStyle = this.projId === 2 ? '#FFB060' : '#D8C8A0';
      ctx.lineWidth = 2;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(0, 0);
      ctx.lineTo(0, -Math.min(14, Math.hypot(this.vx, this.vy) * 1.4));
      ctx.stroke();
    }
    ctx.restore();
  }

  fixedUpdate(_dt: number, game: GameHooks) {
    // 联机傀儡（远端弹幕快照驱动）：纯位移外推，不跑命中/碰撞（伤害归拥有者客户端结算）
    if (this.netPuppet) { this.x += this.vx; this.y += this.vy; return; }
    if (--this.life <= 0) { this.dead = true; return; }
    const world = game.world;
    // aiStyle 1/2 通用重力（原版箭 0.3/tick 抛物线；直飞弹 grav=0）
    if (this.grav !== 0) this.vy = Math.min(this.vy + this.grav, 16);
    this.x += this.vx;
    this.y += this.vy;
    const tx = Math.floor((this.x + this.w / 2) / TILE);
    const ty = Math.floor((this.y + this.h / 2) / TILE);
    if (!world.store.inBounds(tx, ty)) { this.dead = true; return; }
    const tileType = world.store.get(tx, ty);
    if (tileType !== 0) {
      // 可砍物（杂草/瓦罐）：Projectile.CutTiles 语义——弹幕扫过即砍
      game.cutTile(tx, ty);
      // 阻挡判定只看【实心】(Main.tileSolid 语义):树干/火把/平台等非实心格
      // 箭直接穿过(此前 tileType!==0 一刀切,箭会被树挡住——树 solid:false)
      if (!world.store.isSolid(tx, ty)) return;
      // 实心块：1/3 概率回收掉落（原版箭 Kill 的掉落），然后消失
      if (this.dropKey && Math.random() < 1 / 3) game.spawnDrop(this.x, this.y, this.dropKey, 1);
      this.dead = true;
      return;
    }
    // 小动物:一击致死并消耗弹体(原版小动物是 NPC;近战挥砍同语义)
    if (hitCritters(this, game)) { this.dead = true; return; }
    // 命中敌人（原版 penetrate：箭 1 射中即停；手里剑 4 穿 4 敌，同敌免疫防连击）

      const e = ent as unknown as { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; id: number; dead: boolean; hurt: (d: number, kx: number, ky: number, g: GameHooks) => boolean; def?: { hitSound?: string[] } };
      if (e.dead) continue;
      if (this.x < e.x + e.w && this.x + this.w > e.x && this.y < e.y + e.h && this.y + this.h > e.y) {

```ts
// ===================== AI_084 拜月教邪教徒 439 + 克隆体 440(NPC.cs:64921-65957) =====================
// (as above)
const CULTIST_CYCLE_P1 = [0, 1, 0, 2, 0, 3, 0, 1, 0, 2, 0, 4];
const CULTIST_CYCLE_P2 = [0, 1, 0, 5, 0, 3, 0, 5, 0, 2, 0, 3];

/** 选招(:65131-65254 两张 switch 表合一;表尾拍把 ai3 复位 -1 → 下次完成攻击后回 0 重循环) */
function cultistPick(e: Enemy, phase2: boolean): number {
  const table = phase2 ? CULTIST_CYCLE_P2 : CULTIST_CYCLE_P1;
  const i = Math.floor(e.ai3);
  if (i < 0 || i >= table.length) { e.ai3 = -1; return 0; }
  if (i === table.length - 1) e.ai3 = -1; // :65200/:65254 表尾复位
  return table[i];
}

/** 真身的全部克隆体(:65449 系:type==440 && ai[3]==whoAmI;本仓以 localAI 槽 la3 存真身引用) */
function cultistClones(game: GameHooks, parent: Enemy): Enemy[] {
  return (game.enemies() as Enemy[]).filter(
    (n) => !n.dead && n.vanillaId === 440 && laOf(n).la3 === parent);
}

/** 克隆体生成出口(:65646-65650 NewNPC 440:X,Y 为底部中心,Y 再 +height/2;
 *  ai[3]=whoAmI / localAI[1]=代数)。本仓:la3=真身引用,la1=代数,跳过出生显影 */
function spawnCultistClone(game: GameHooks, parent: Enemy, x: number, y: number, gen: number): Enemy | null {
  const c = spawnPart(game, parent, 440);
  if (!c) return null;
  const la = laOf(c);
  la.la3 = parent;
  la.la1 = gen;
  c.x = x - c.w / 2;
  c.y = y + parent.h / 2 - c.h;
  c.bInit = true;           // 跳过出生显影(原版克隆体仅 1t 落入 -1 态,无实际效果)
  return c;
}

/** 克隆体补射 468(:65462-65499 火球第三拍 / :65531-65562 闪电 / :65796-65830 光第三拍):
 *  从克隆体中心+facing*30,+12 发一发(或 n 发)18 伤光弹,6+rand4 速 ±spread 散布 */
function cloneSupplement(game: GameHooks, clones: Enemy[], player: Player, count: number, spread: number): void {
  for (const c of clones) {
    const dir = Math.sign(player.cx - c.cx);
    if (dir !== 0) c.facing = dir;
    const aim = aimLead(c, player, c.facing);
    for (let k = 0; k < count; k++) {
      const spd = 6 + Math.random() * 4;
      const [dvx, dvy] = rot(aim.x * spd, aim.y * spd, (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2 * spread);
      shoot(game, c.cx + c.facing * 30, c.cy + 12, dvx, dvy, 18, 468);
    }
  }
}
```

// :65066 alpha -= 5/tick\n    e.iframes = 2; // flag3/flag4 → dontTakeDamage\n    e.ai1 += 1;\n    if (e.ai1 > 360) { e.vx *= 0.95; e.vy *= 0.95; }      // :65117-65123 收势阻尼\n    else if (e.ai1 > 300) { e.vx = 0; e.vy = -1; }        // :65126-65130 velocity=-UnitY\n    flyIntegrate(e);\n    if (e.ai1 >= 420) { e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; e.spawnAlpha = 0; }\n    return;\n  }\n\n  // ---- ai0 == 0 选招(:65110-65336):40t 后按 ai3 拍号取招 ----\n  if (e.ai0 === 0) {\n    e.ai1 += 1;\n    if (e.ai1 >= 40) {\n      e.ai1 = 0;\n      const pick = cultistPick(e, phase2);\n      if (pick === 0) {\n        // 飞掠占位(:65277-65336):无克隆体时环位恒为玩家正上 (0,-200)(num16=0),\n        // num14 = ceil(距离/50) 至少 1;velocity = 位移/num14,ai1 = num14*2(隔拍移动)\n        const destX = player.cx;\n        const destY = player.cy - 200;\n        const dist = Math.hypot(destX - e.cx, destY - e.cy);\n        const num14 = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(dist / 50));\n        e.vx = (destX - e.cx) / num14;\n        e.vy = (destY - e.cy) / num14;\n        e.x -= e.vx; e.y -= e.vy; // :65318-65321 whoAmI>=自身 → 选拍当帧回退(净位移 0)\n        e.ai0 = 1;\n        e.ai1 = num14 * 2;\n      } else if (pick === 1) { e.ai0 = 3; } // 火球\n      else if (pick === 2) { e.ai0 = 2; }   // 冰雾\n      else if (pick === 3) { e.ai0 = 4; }   // 闪电\n      else if (pick === 4) { e.ai0 = 5; }   // 仪式瞬移\n      else if (pick === 5) { e.ai0 = 7; }   // 远古之光\n      // pick === 6(远古末日)仅 expert 随机替换会命中(:65270-65275),经典不触发\n    }\n    flyIntegrate(e);\n    return;\n  }\n\n  // ---- ai0 == 1 飞掠(:65338-65361):奇数拍回退位移 → 实际隔拍移动;期间无敌(flag3) ----\n  if (e.ai0 === 1) {\n    e.iframes = 2;\n    const undo = Math.floor(e.ai1) % 2 !== 0 && e.ai1 !== 1; // :65341-65344\n    if (!undo) flyIntegrate(e);\n    e.ai1 -= 1;\n    if (e.ai1 <= 0) { e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai3 += 1; e.vx = 0; e.vy = 0; }\n    return;\n  }\n\n  // ---- ai0 == 2 冰雾 464(:65362-65429):4t 时一发,35 伤,4 速直飞;持续 4+120 ----\n  if (e.ai0 === 2) {\n    if (e.ai1 >= 4 && (e.ai1 - 4) % 120 === 0) {\n      const aim = aimLead(e, player, e.facing);\n      const [ox, oy] = [e.cx + e.facing * 30, e.cy + 12];\n      shoot(game, ox, oy, aim.x * 4, aim.y * 4, 35, 464);\n      game.playSfx('roar', 0.35);\n    }\n    e.ai1 += 1;\n    if (e.ai1 >= 4 + 120) { e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai3 += 1; e.vx = 0; e.vy = 0; }\n    return;\n  }\n\n  // ---- ai0 == 3 火球 467(:65430-65530):每 18t 一发×3,30 伤,(6+rand4) 速 ±0.52rad 散布;持续 4+18*3 ----\n  if (e.ai0 === 3) {\n    if (e.ai1 >= 4 && (e.ai1 - 4) % 18 === 0) {\n      const aim = aimLead(e, player, e.facing);\n      const spd = 6 + Math.random() * 4;\n      const [dvx, dvy] = rot(aim.x * spd, aim.y * spd, (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2 * 0.5235987901687622);\n      shoot(game, e.cx + e.facing * 30, e.cy + 12, dvx, dvy, 30, 467);\n    }\n    // 第三拍(num13==2)克隆体补射 468(:65462-65499)——无克隆体,跳过\n    e.ai1 += 1;\n    if (e.ai1 >= 4 + 18 * 3) { e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai3 += 1; e.vx = 0; e.vy = 0; }\n    return;\n  }\n\n  // ---- ai0 == 4 闪电 465(:65531-65622):20t 时在头顶 100px 撒球,45 伤;持续 20+80 ----\n  if (e.ai0 === 4) {\n    if (e.ai1 === 20) {\n      // 原版 465 零速悬停后从天引雷(Projectile.AI 收敛:直飞 6 速朝玩家)\n      const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - (e.cy - 100);\n      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;\n      shoot(game, e.cx, e.cy - 100, dx / d * 6, dy / d * 6, 45, 465);\n      game.playSfx('roar', 0.5);\n    }\n    e.ai1 += 1;\n    if (e.ai1 >= 20 + 80) { e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai3 += 1; e.vx = 0; e.vy = 0; }\n    return;\n  }\n\n  // ---- ai0 == 5 仪式瞬移(:65623-65756):0-30 淡出,30 瞬移,30-90 隐形,90-120 淡入,\n  //      120-420 可被选中(打真身窗口);持续 420 ----\n  if (e.ai0 === 5) {\n    const t = e.ai1;\n    if (t >= 0 && t < 30) {\n      e.spawnAlpha = Math.floor((t / 30) * 254);\n      e.iframes = 2; // flag3/flag4\n    } else if (t >= 30 && t < 90) {\n      e.spawnAlpha = 254;\n      e.iframes = 2;\n      if (t === 30) {\n        // :65636-65648 原版为绕自身半径 180 的环位重定位(≤180px);\n        // 收敛:直接瞬移到绕玩家椭圆 (300,200) 8 环位中最远一格——即下一拍飞掠的目的地,\n        // 保留「消失→别处再现」语义且产生 >300px 位置跳变。\n        let far = 0, farD = -1;\n        for (let k = 0; k < 8; k++) {\n          const a = (k / 8) * Math.PI * 2;\n          const px = player.cx + Math.cos(a) * 300;\n          const py = player.cy + Math.sin(a) * 200;\n          const d = Math.hypot(px - e.cx, py - e.cy);\n          if (d > farD) { farD = d; far = k; }\n        }\n        const a = (far / 8) * Math.PI * 2;\n        e.x = player.cx + Math.cos(a) * 300 - e.w / 2;\n        e.y = player.cy + Math.sin(a) * 200 - e.h / 2;\n        e.vx = 0; e.vy = 0;\n        game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy, '#8060D0', 18, 2.6);\n        game.playSfx('roar', 0.7);\n        // 克隆体(NPC 440,环位补位 + 490 仪式弹幕,:65636-65675)按任务要求跳过\n      }\n    } else if (t >= 90 && t < 120) {\n      e.spawnAlpha = Math.floor(254 * (1 - (t - 90) / 30));\n      e.iframes = 2;\n    } else {\n      e.spawnAlpha = 0; // 120-420 可选中(chaseable)\n    }\n    e.ai1 += 1;\n    if (e.ai1 >= 420) { e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai3 += 1; e.vx = 0; e.vy = 0; }\n    return;\n  }\n\n  // ---- ai0 == 6 打中真身硬直(:65757-65770,克隆分支专用)——跳过 ----\n\n  // ---- ai0 == 7 远古之光(:65796-65877):每 20t 一轮×2,每轮 5 发呈扇面(步进 2π/25);持续 4+20*2 ----\n  if (e.ai0 === 7) {\n    if (e.ai1 >= 4 && (e.ai1 - 4) % 20 === 0) {\n      e.facing = Math.sign(player.cx - e.cx) || e.facing; // :65842-65846\n      const aim = aimLead(e, player, e.facing);\n      const num47 = (Math.PI * 2) / 25; // :65856\n      for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {\n        const [dvx, dvy] = rot(aim.x * 8, aim.y * 8, num47 * i - (Math.PI * 2 / 5 - num47) / 2); // :65860\n        // 简化项:原版生成 5 只 NPC522 远古之光(:65862-65865,aiStyle 100 直飞核未移植,\n        // 现分发会落 zombieAI),按同弹道收敛为 468 直飞弹,伤取同段克隆火球 18(:65837)\n        shoot(game, e.cx + e.facing * 30, e.cy + 12 + 7, dvx, dvy, 18, 468);\n      }\n      game.playSfx('roar', 0.5);\n    }\n    e.ai1 += 1;\n    if (e.ai1 >= 4 + 20 * 2) { e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai3 += 1; e.vx = 0; e.vy = 0; }\n    return;\n  }\n\n  // ---- ai0 == 8 远古末日(:65873-65932):玩家附近随机空位撒 NPC523(最多 1 只);持续 4+20*3 ----\n  // 经典选招表不含 6(expert 随机替换专属,:65270-65275),此处仅保留状态体防外部置入。\n  if (e.ai0 === 8) {\n    if (e.ai1 >= 4 && (e.ai1 - 4) % 20 === 0) {\n      const st = game.world.store;\n      const ptx = Math.floor(player.cx / TILE), pty = Math.floor(player.cy / TILE);\n      const etx = Math.floor(e.cx / TILE), ety = Math.floor(e.cy / TILE);\n      for (let tries = 0; tries < 100; tries++) {\n        const tx = ptx + Math.floor(Math.random() * 41) - 20;\n        const ty = pty + Math.floor(Math.random() * 41) - 20;\n        const nearPlayer = Math.abs(ty - pty) <= 7 && Math.abs(tx - ptx) <= 7;\n        const nearSelf = Math.abs(ty - ety) <= 3 && Math.abs(tx - etx) <= 3;\n        if (nearPlayer || nearSelf || !st.inBounds(tx, ty) || st.isSolid(tx, ty)) continue;\n        const n = spawnPart(game, e, 523);\n        if (n) { n.x = tx * TILE + 8 - n.w / 2; n.y = ty * TILE + 8 - n.h / 2; n.vx = 0; n.vy = 0; n.bInit = true; }\n        break;\n      }\n    }\n    e.ai1 += 1;\n    if (e.ai1 >= 4 + 20 * 3) { e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai3 += 1; e.vx = 0; e.vy = 0; }\n    return;\n  }\n\n  // 未知状态:回选招\n  e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0;\n}",
 "new_string": "// ===================== AI_084 拜月教邪教徒 439 + 克隆体 440(NPC.cs:64921-65957) =====================\n// 真身 439 与克隆体 440 共用本核(flag2 = type==439,:64971):克隆体由仪式态(ai0==5)生成,\n//   ai[0]/ai[1] 每 tick 从真身镜像(:65000-65001);仅在仪式窗口(120-420t)可被击中——\n//   打中克隆体 → 克隆体消散 + 真身进 6 态硬直 120t(:65003-65014);\n//   打中真身 → 推进选招拍号并清同代克隆体(:65016-65060)。\n//   仪式态外克隆体无敌且不可选(dontTakeDamage=flag3,:65953)。\n// 状态机(ai0): -1 出生显形(420t,真身专属) / 0 选招(40t) / 1 飞掠占位(含克隆体环位补位) /\n//   2 冰雾 464(:65362) / 3 火球 467(:65430,第三拍克隆体补射 468) / 4 闪电 465(:65531) /\n//   5 仪式瞬移(:65623,生成/补位克隆体) / 6 硬直(:65771) / 7 远古之光(:65796,真身召\n//   NPC522 轨道弹 + 第三拍克隆体补射 468×5) / 8 远古末日 NPC523(经典不可达,状态体保留)。\n// 经典数值: 冰 35 伤(:64930)/火 30 伤·18t×3(:64941-64946)/闪电 45 伤(:64953)/\n//   光 20t×2(:64963-64967)/末日 20t×3(:64970-64973);克隆体补射 468 恒 18 伤(:65491)。\n// 选招表(:65131-65254 两张 switch 表合一;表尾拍把 ai3 复位 -1 → 下次完成攻击后回 0 重循环):\n//   一阶段 12 拍 [冲,火,冲,冰,冲,雷,冲,火,冲,冰,冲,仪式] 循环;\n//   二阶段 12 拍 [冲,火,冲,光,冲,雷,冲,光,冲,冰,冲,雷] 循环(二阶段不再仪式)。\nconst CULTIST_CYCLE_P1 = [0, 1, 0, 2, 0, 3, 0, 1, 0, 2, 0, 4];\nconst CULTIST_CYCLE_P2 = [0, 1, 0, 5, 0, 3, 0, 5, 0, 2, 0, 3];\n\n/** 选招(:65131-65254 两张 switch 表合一;表尾拍把 ai3 复位 -1 → 下次完成攻击后回 0 重循环) */\nfunction cultistPick(e: Enemy, phase2: boolean): number {\n  const table = phase2 ? CULTIST_CYCLE_P2 : CULTIST_CYCLE_P1;\n  const i = Math.floor(e.ai3);\n  if (i < 0 || i >= table.length) { e.ai3 = -1; return 0; }\n  if (i === table.length - 1) e.ai3 = -1; // :65199/:65253 表尾复位\n  return table[i];\n}\n\n/** 真身的全部存活克隆体(:65449 系:type==440 && ai[3]==whoAmI;\n *  本仓克隆体以 localAI 槽 la3 反存真身 Enemy 引用) */\nfunction cultistClones(game: GameHooks, parent: Enemy): Enemy[] {\n  return (game.enemies() as Enemy[]).filter(\n    (n) => !n.dead && n.vanillaId === 440 && laOf(n).la3 === parent);\n}\n\n/** 克隆体生成出口(:65646-65650 NewNPC 440:X,Y 是底部中心坐标且 Y 再 +真身 height/2;\n *  ai[3]=真身 whoAmI / localAI[1]=仪式代数)。本仓:la3=真身引用、la1=代数、跳过出生显影 */\nfunction spawnCultistClone(game: GameHooks, parent: Enemy, x: number, y: number, gen: number): Enemy | null {\n  const c = spawnPart(game, parent, 440);\n  if (!c) return null;\n  const la = laOf(c);\n  la.la3 = parent;\n  la.la1 = gen;\n  c.x = x - c.w / 2;\n  c.y = y + parent.h / 2 - c.h;\n  c.bInit = true; // 原版克隆体会落入 1t 的 -1 显影态(:65047),无实际效果,直接跳过\n  return c;\n}\n\n/** 克隆体补射 468(:65375-65397 冰雾 / :65462-65499 火球第三拍 / :65531-65562 闪电 /\n *  :65796-65822 光第三拍):每只克隆体朝玩家(预判 20t)发 count 发,18 伤,\n *  (6+rand4) 速 ±spread 散布,出口 = 克隆体中心 + (facing*30, 12) */\nfunction cloneSupplement(game: GameHooks, clones: Enemy[], player: Player, count: number, spread: number): void {\n  for (const c of clones) {\n    const dir = Math.sign(player.cx - c.cx);\n    if (dir !== 0) c.facing = dir;\n    const aim = aimLead(c, player, c.facing);\n    for (let k = 0; k < count; k++) {\n      const spd = 6 + Math.random() * 4;\n      const [dvx, dvy] = rot(aim.x * spd, aim.y * spd, (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2 * spread);\n      shoot(game, c.cx + c.facing * 30, c.cy + 12, dvx, dvy, 18, 468);\n    }\n  }\n}\n\n/** 仪式圈心(原版 Projectile 490 的位置 = 仪式开始时真身中心,:65652)。\n *  真身存 la2;克隆体读真身的 la2 */\nfunction ritualCenterOf(isReal: boolean, la: { la2: number }, parent: Enemy | null): { x: number; y: number } | null {\n  const raw = isReal ? la.la2 : (parent ? laOf(parent).la2 : 0);\n  return raw ? (raw as unknown as { x: number; y: number }) : null;\n}\n\nexport function lunaticCultistAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null): void {\n  const la = laOf(e);\n  const isReal = e.vanillaId !== 440; // flag2 = type==439(:64971);440 = 克隆体\n  let parent: Enemy | null = null;\n  // ---- 克隆体镜像段(:64998-65015):真身失效 → 消散;ai0/ai1 逐 tick 镜像;\n  //      仪式态中被击中 → 自身消散 + 真身进 6 态硬直 ----\n  if (!isReal) {\n    parent = (la.la3 as unknown as Enemy) ?? null;\n    if (!parent || parent.dead || parent.vanillaId !== 439) { // :64975-64982\n      e.dead = true;\n      return;\n    }\n    e.ai0 = parent.ai0;\n    e.ai1 = parent.ai1;\n    if (e.ai0 === 5) {\n      if (e.justHit) { // :65003-65014\n        game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy, '#8060D0', 14, 2.4);\n        e.dead = true;\n        parent.ai0 = 6;\n        parent.ai1 = 0;\n        return;\n      }\n    } else {\n      e.iframes = 2; // flag3/flag4 → dontTakeDamage(chaseable=false,:65015)\n    }\n  } else if (!e.bInit) {\n    // :65047-65057 localAI[0]==0:音效 + alpha=255 + ai[0]=-1(出生显形)\n    e.bInit = true;\n    e.ai0 = -1; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 = 0;\n    la.la0 = 1;\n    e.spawnAlpha = 254;\n    game.playSfx('roar', 0.6);\n  }\n  const b = baseOf(e);\n  const phase2 = e.hp <= e.maxHp / 2;                       // flag :64928\n  e.def.defense = Math.floor(phase2 ? b.def * 0.65 : b.def); // :64982-64985 半血防御 ×0.65\n\n  // :65060-65088 玩家死/超 5600px → 消散退场(连带清全部克隆体,:65078-65087)\n  if (!player || player.dead || Math.hypot(player.cx - e.cx, player.cy - e.cy) > 5600) {\n    game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy, '#6040A0', 14, 2.4);\n    if (isReal) {\n      for (const c of cultistClones(game, e)) {\n        game.spawnParticles(c.cx, c.cy, '#6040A0', 10, 2);\n        c.dead = true;\n      }\n    }\n    e.dead = true;\n    return;\n  }\n\n  // ---- 仪式窗口(120-420t)真身被击中(:65016-65060):推进选招 + 清同代克隆体(≤10) ----\n  if (isReal && e.ai0 === 5 && e.ai1 >= 120 && e.ai1 < 420 && e.justHit) {\n    e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai3 += 1; // :65021-65025\n    e.vx = 0; e.vy = 0;\n    let budget = 10; // num9 经典 10(expert 3,:65041-65044);两分支同为清场,差异仅联网消息\n    for (const c of cultistClones(game, e)) {\n      if (budget <= 0) break;\n      budget--;\n      game.spawnParticles(c.cx, c.cy, '#8060D0', 12, 2.4);\n      c.dead = true;\n    }\n  }\n\n  // 朝向(:65117-65120 sign(player.X - center.X);仪式态内被 :65676 系圈心朝向覆盖)\n  const face = Math.sign(player.cx - e.cx);\n  if (face !== 0) e.facing = face;\n\n  // ---- ai0 == -1 出生显形(:65059-65109):420t,前段不可选中;300t 起上浮 ----\n  if (e.ai0 === -1) {\n    e.spawnAlpha = Math.max(0, 254 - e.ai1 * 5); // :65066 alpha -= 5/tick\n    e.iframes = 2; // flag3/flag4 → dontTakeDamage\n    e.ai1 += 1;\n    if (e.ai1 > 360) { e.vx *= 0.95; e.vy *= 0.95; }      // :65109-65111 收势阻尼\n    else if (e.ai1 > 300) { e.vx = 0; e.vy = -1; }        // :65113-65115 velocity=-UnitY\n    flyIntegrate(e);\n    if (e.ai1 >= 420) { e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; e.spawnAlpha = 0; }\n    return;\n  }\n\n  // ---- ai0 == 0 选招(:65110-65336):40t 后按 ai3 拍号取招(flag2 门控,克隆体不选) ----\n  if (e.ai0 === 0) {\n    e.ai1 += 1;\n    if (isReal && e.ai1 >= 40) {\n      e.ai1 = 0;\n      const pick = cultistPick(e, phase2);\n      if (pick === 0) {\n        // 飞掠占位(:65277-65336):真身 + 全部克隆体一起入环——环位角\n        // num16 = ((序号+flag5+1)/2)·2π·0.4/总数,奇数序取负(flag5=总数偶数),\n        // 半径椭圆 (300,200),自转轴 (0,-1);num14 = ceil(距离/50) 至少 1,\n        // velocity = 位移/num14,ai1 = num14*2(隔拍移动;克隆体 ai1 会被镜像覆写为真身值)\n        const group = [e, ...cultistClones(game, e)];\n        const flag5 = group.length % 2 === 0 ? 1 : 0; // :65287\n        let idx = 0;\n        for (const m of group) {\n          let num16 = Math.floor((idx + flag5 + 1) / 2) * Math.PI * 2 * 0.4 / group.length; // :65291-65296\n          if (idx % 2 === 1) num16 = -num16;\n          if (group.length === 1) num16 = 0;\n          const [ux, uy] = rot(0, -1, num16);\n          const destX = player.cx + ux * 300;\n          const destY = player.cy + uy * 200;\n          const num14 = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(Math.hypot(destX - m.cx, destY - m.cy) / 50)); // :65279-65283\n          m.vx = (destX - m.cx) / num14;\n          m.vy = (destY - m.cy) / num14;\n          m.ai0 = 1;\n          m.ai1 = num14 * 2;\n          // :65318-65321 whoAmI >= 自身 → 选招当帧回退(净位移 0);克隆体按 id 序决定\n          if (m === e || e.id >= m.id) { m.x -= m.vx; The la record type is `{ la0: number; la1: number; la2: number; la3: number }`. I stored an Enemy in la3 and an object in la2. Need to loosen the type. Change localAI record type to `{ la0: number; la1: number; la2: unknown; la3: unknown }`? That affects other users: la.la0 = e.maxHp (number), la.la1 spot coords (numbers), la.la2, la.la3. Simplest: change the WeakMap type to a permissive record:

```ts
const localAI = new WeakMap<Enemy, { la0: number; la1: number; la2: unknown; la3: unknown }>();
```

But then queenSlimeAI does `la.la1 = spot.x; la.la2 = spot.y;` — assigning number to unknown is fine. And reads `la.la1 !== 0` fine.

Hmm, but other files? localAI is private to this file. Also ancientDoom uses la? No. Let me check usages: laOf(e).la0..la3 — cultist (la0 init flag, la1 gen, la2 ritual center obj, la3 parent Enemy), queen (la0 hp mark, la1/la2 teleport spot). So type `{ la0: number; la1: number; la2: unknown; la3: unknown }` works. Line 139 `la.la3 = parent` → unknown OK. Line 130 `parent = (la.la3 as unknown as Enemy) ?? null` OK.

Also the ritualCenterOf signature uses `la: { la2: number }` — change to unknown.

The other errors (TileRunner, tests) are pre-existing from parallel agents (在制品) — task says "tsc 零新增（忽略在制品）".
````

 "new_string": "/** 朝玩家发射一发弹幕的通用出口(伤害/弹 id 全部来自原版 NewProjectile 调用点) */\nfunction mlShoot(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player, ox: number, oy: number,\n  spd: number, dmg: number, projId: number) {\n  const dx = player.cx - (e.cx + ox), dy = player.cy - (e.cy + oy);\n  const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;\n  shoot(game, e.cx + ox, e.cy + oy, (dx / d) * spd, (dy / d) * spd, dmg, projId, 600);\n}\n\n/** checkDead(:78845-78857):手 397 / 头 396 血尽 → ai0=-2 闭眼残肢的同时,于部件中心\n *  召 NPC 400 真眼,ai[3] = 部件的 ai[3](核心 id,游离眼 AI 据此锚定核心) */\nfunction spawnTrueEye(game: GameHooks, part: Enemy): void {\n  const eye = spawnPart(game, part, 400);\n  if (!eye) return;\n  eye.x = part.cx - eye.w / 2;\n  eye.y = part.cy - eye.h / 2;\n  eye.ai0 = 0; eye.ai1 = 0; eye.ai2 = 0; eye.ai3 = part.ai3;\n}\n\n/** 月噬怪召出(:37828-37842 残肢 / :37975-37989 月噬攻击):头在 lt/inSlot=120/180/240\n *  于玩家处召 NPC 401,ai[0]=头 whoAmI+1 / ai[1]=源弹 456 索引。\n *  原版门禁 = 玩家身中 MoonLeech debuff 145(由 456 月噬弹命中赋予);本仓 456 为\n *  0 伤纯视觉弹且 debuff 145 未实装——按「月噬弹已射出即视为命中」无条件补召\n *  (ai[1] 源弹索引以 -1 代,moonLeechAI 以出生点代弹着点,不受影响)。 */\nfunction spawnMoonLeech(game: GameHooks, head: Enemy, player: Player): void {\n  const leech = spawnPart(game, head, 401);\n  if (!leech) return;\n  leech.x = player.cx - leech.w / 2;\n  leech.y = player.cy - leech.h / 2;\n  leech.ai0 = head.id + 1;\n  leech.ai1 = -1;\n  leech.ai2 = 0;\n  leech.vx = 0; leech.vy = 0;\n}"

// 月系批 AI 家族探针（aiStyle 81/82/83/86/87/88/89/90/91 —— bossAI_lunar_misc.ts）：
// 状态机主干 / 弹幕节奏 / 跨实体联动（月总核心锚定、石碑→教徒→邪教徒链、卵→幼蛾 Transform）。
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { Enemy } from '../src/entities/Enemy';
import { World } from '../src/world/World';
import { TILE } from '../src/core/constants';
import type { GameHooks } from '../src/entities/types';

const W = 160, H = 120, FLOOR = 100;

function makeWorld(walls = false): World {
  const w = new World(W, H, 7, 'lunar-misc-test');
  const st = w.store;
  w.groundLevel = 50;
  for (let x = 0; x < W; x++) {
    st.setTile(x, FLOOR, 1);
    st.setTile(x, FLOOR + 1, 1);
  }
  if (walls) {
    for (let y = FLOOR - 20; y < FLOOR; y++) st.setTile(70, y, 1);   // 玩家眼前隔墙
  }
  return w;
}

type Proj = { damage: number; vx: number; vy: number; projId: number };

function makeHooks(world: World, playerAt: { x: number; y: number }) {
  const enemies: Enemy[] = [];
  const projectiles: Proj[] = [];
  const em = {
    nextId: 1,
    add: (e: unknown, bucket: 'enemies' | 'projectiles') => {
      const ent = e as { id: number };
      ent.id = em.nextId++;
      if (bucket === 'enemies') enemies.push(e as Enemy);
      else projectiles.push(e as Proj);
    },
  };
  const px = playerAt.x * TILE, py = playerAt.y * TILE;
  const player = {
    x: px, y: py, w: 20, h: 42, cx: px + 10, cy: py + 21,
    vx: 0, vy: 0, hp: 400, maxHp: 400, dead: false, useTime: 1,
    aabbOverlaps: () => false,
  };
  const hooks = {
    world, player,
    enemies: () => enemies,
    critters: () => [],
    spawnDrop: () => null,
    damagePlayer: () => {},
    addDamageNumber: () => {},
    cutTile: () => {},
    onEnemyKilled: () => {},
    spawnEnemy: () => {},
    spawnParticles: () => {},
    notifyInventoryChanged: () => {},
    playSfx: () => {},
    playSfxFiles: () => {},
    showPickupLabel: () => {},
    entities: em,

function spawn(h: ReturnType<typeof makeHooks>, id: number, tx: number, ty: number): Enemy {
  const e = Enemy.fromVanilla(id, tx * TILE, ty * TILE)!;
  e.id = h.em.nextId++;
  h.enemies.push(e);
  return e;
}

/** 逐 tick 驱动指定实体列表（默认全部敌人） */
function tick(h: ReturnType<typeof makeHooks>, n: number, only?: Enemy[]) {
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    for (const e of only ?? h.enemies) {
      if (!e.dead) e.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, h.hooks);
    }
  }
}

describe('AI_081 月总游离眼 400', () => {
  it('攻击表十槽循环：90t 进矢槽，d-14/-7 两连幻影矢（35 伤；t=d 槽切同帧不可达，原版同）', () => {
    const w = makeWorld();
    const h = makeHooks(w, { x: 80, y: 96 });
    const core = spawn(h, 398, 80, 90);
    core.bInit = true; core.ai0 = 0;
    const eye = spawn(h, 400, 40, 88);
    eye.ai3 = core.id; eye.ai0 = 0; eye.ai1 = 0;
    tick(h, 175, [eye]);
    expect(eye.ai0).toBe(1);                          // 悬停 90t 后进矢槽(t=85)
    expect(h.projectiles.filter((p) => p.damage === 35).length).toBe(2);   // t=d-14/-7 两发
    tick(h, 40, [eye]);
    expect(eye.ai0).toBe(0);                          // 180t 回悬停槽
    expect(eye.cy).toBeLessThan(h.player.cy);         // 悬停玩家上方
  });

  it('核心 398 消亡 → 游离眼静默消散（无掉落管线）', () => {
    const w = makeWorld();
    const h = makeHooks(w, { x: 80, y: 96 });
    const core = spawn(h, 398, 80, 90);
    const eye = spawn(h, 400, 40, 88);
    eye.ai3 = core.id; eye.ai0 = 0; eye.ai1 = 0;
    tick(h, 30, [eye]);
    expect(eye.dead).toBe(false);
    core.dead = true;
    tick(h, 2, [eye]);
    expect(eye.dead).toBe(true);
  });
});

describe('AI_082 月总水蛭块 401', () => {
